- **socratic-elenchus**: Socratic dialogue (elenchus) as in Plato's Socratic dialogues. Finds the concept an idea stands on, asks "what is X?", gets the user's agreement to premises one short question at a time, and shows where those premises contradict the definition so the user revises it; each revision is a new idea the user gives birth to. Usually ends in aporia (knowing what you don't know). Gives no advice or ideas of its own. Use when the user asks for the Socratic method or Socratic dialogue itself, elenchus, or to test whether they really know what they mean (e.g. "socratic dialogue", "elenchus", "what do I… ([ajitta/dialectic-skills](https://github.com/ajitta/dialectic-skills/tree/HEAD/upstream/superclaude-stable-47f791b/socratic-elenchus) · ★2)

- **socratic-gate**: Socratic questioning protocol + user communication. MANDATORY for complex requests, new features, or unclear requirements. Includes progress reporting and error handling. (Formerly `brainstorming`.) ([SKB3002/agenthub](https://github.com/SKB3002/agenthub/tree/HEAD/plugins/hub/skills/socratic-gate) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **socratic-grilling**: Grill the user to build a shared mental model. ([lyndonkl/hermesworld](https://github.com/lyndonkl/hermesworld/tree/HEAD/packages/exploratory-strategist/skills/cognitive-scaffolds/socratic-grilling) · 2 copies)
- **socratic-grilling-patterns**: Fornece algoritmo de árvore de decisão e protocolo de registro concorrente (Grill-with-Docs) para conduzir interrogatório socrático estruturado via ask_questions, mapeando a fronteira ativa de incerteza até decisões arquiteturais documentadas. Use quando um requisito, decisão arquitetural ou trade-off técnico permanecer ambíguo após a primeira rodada de clarificação. Não use para coleta trivial de dados objetivos — usar structured-intake-patterns. ([jezreel8858/deep-agents-copilot](https://github.com/jezreel8858/deep-agents-copilot/tree/HEAD/.github/skills/socratic-grilling-patterns) · ★3)
- **socratic-ground-up**: Guides the learner to understand an unfamiliar topic from absolute zero through Socratic questioning, instead of explaining it to them. First builds a complete dependency graph of every concept involved and shows it as a map, then walks the learner through it with very small, easy questions (often obvious ones) so they reason out each idea themselves, brick by brick, while the map recolours to show what is done and what is left. Use this whenever the user wants to learn by working it out, asks to be guided or quizzed into understanding, says things like "don't just tell me, guide me", "make m… ([matteosissa/personal-skills-marketplace](https://github.com/matteosissa/personal-skills-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/deep-explainer/skills/socratic-ground-up))
- **socratic-inquiry**: 苏格拉底式问诊提示语原文。用户说不清自己到底想问什么、问题一团乱、想先把问题问清楚再动手时使用。触发词包括 苏格拉底、苏格拉底式提问、帮我问诊、帮我理清问题、我不知道该问什么、先别给建议、我到底想问什么。一次只问一个问题，最多六问，最后产出一个真正值得回答的新问题。问完要出三版方案，用 align-then-three。要直接核查问题定义并指出更值得解决的问题，用 challenge-the-question。 ([LearnPrompt/laoyeye](https://github.com/LearnPrompt/laoyeye/tree/HEAD/skills/asking/socratic-inquiry) · ★110)
- **socratic-inquiry**: Use one-question-at-a-time Socratic dialogue to clarify dilemmas, emotional loops, hidden assumptions, conflicting goals, and self-understanding. Use when the user wants to think through a problem rather than receive an immediate solution. ([kaluli123123/top-thinking](https://github.com/kaluli123123/top-thinking/tree/HEAD/top-thinking/skills/socratic-inquiry))
- **socratic-inquiry**: Use when a request turns on an abstract quality word — reliable, scalable, secure, robust, maintainable, clean, fast, simple, better, production-ready — and nobody has said what would count as achieving it, so acting would mean deciding for yourself what the word means. Also use when a cause is asserted rather than demonstrated ("the bug is X, go fix X"), or when a question presupposes its own answer. Not for choosing between named options — that is scholastic-disputation. ([sudeepgupta90/foundational-thinking](https://github.com/sudeepgupta90/foundational-thinking/tree/HEAD/skills/socratic-inquiry) · 2 copies)
- **socratic-interrogation**: Subject proposals, specs, and council arguments to disciplined Socratic interrogation with minimalist, probing questions. Use when evaluating critical design premises or high-contention claims. ([jessevondoom/delphi](https://github.com/jessevondoom/delphi/tree/HEAD/skills/socratic-interrogation))
- **socratic-interrogator**: Probes ambiguous specifications, hidden assumptions, and underspecified product requests through structured inquiry. ([codebygarv/Ai-skills](https://github.com/codebygarv/Ai-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/reasoning/socratic-interrogator) · ★28)
- **socratic-interview**: Socratic maieutic design interview, driven by structured multiple-choice questions. When a developer brings a vague idea, feature, or design problem, sharpen it into clear decisions by posing ONE focused AskUserQuestion at a time — each option carrying its own reasoning ("X — because …") so the choice captures both the decision and its why. The options are a frame to think against, never a verdict; the choice, and the "Other" escape hatch, are always the developer's. The pen stays in their hand. - **socratic-interview**: Triggers: 苏格拉底提问 / 帮我把问题问清楚 / 我的困惑是...但说不清 / 我到底想问什么. Before answering, interview the user with up to 6 one-at-a-time questions to surface the REAL question, then output a confirmed actionable question before giving any advice.
